Evidence gathered, hypothesis by hypothesis: (1) real Groq for "plan a lunch" (11 samples across 6 phrasings) returns `time_window:"unspecified"/"today, lunchtime"`, `categories:["lunch"]` — no hallucinated clock strings; every sane variant resolves to same-day noon. (2) A TZ=UTC server (mis-deployed Vercel) produces *tomorrow 8 AM Toronto* — wrong, but not midnight. (3) Hallucinated midnight strings ("12am"/"00:00") DO resolve to midnight but the plausible-band check REFUSES them — current code cannot render a midnight itinerary server-side, and `origin/main` = the deployed tip, so the live build has that guard. Eliminating all of those left exactly one mechanism, then proven directly: `app/lib/timeLabels.ts` formatted every label with viewer-local `toLocaleTimeString`/local calendar math — noon Toronto (16:00Z) renders as "4:00 PM" for a UTC viewer (probe output) and as "tomorrow, 12:00 AM" for a UTC+8 viewer: a literal midnight itinerary, 100% reproducible for that viewer, and untouched by all three resolver-side fix attempts because the resolver was never wrong. Fix: timeLabels now pins every label to the plan's home timezone (America/Toronto) — `timeOnly`/`shortDate` via `timeZone:` option, `dayOffset` via Toronto calendar days (Intl formatToParts) — so all viewers read the same Toronto times; test fixtures rewritten as explicit EDT instants so the suite is runner-TZ-independent, plus a pinned regression case (noon EDT → "12:00 PM" for every viewer; the suite is run under TZ=UTC as the remote-viewer simulation). Contract tests added to schedule.test.ts regardless of already passing: both mentor repros, roll-forward on the day-part AND category branches, no-signal/no-category → next full hour. KNOWN INTERACTION flagged for Phase 2 (not fixed here): late at night the immediate next-full-hour slot falls outside the generic 8–23 band, so a vague prompt at ~11:30 PM is refused by resolveStartTimeChecked — pinned in a test comment + assertion. Residual honesty: I cannot see the mentor's browser TZ from here, so "mentor is in a UTC+8-ish zone" is inference — but every server-side path is excluded by direct evidence, and the display flaw is real and wrong for ANY non-Toronto viewer regardless. **ACTION ITEM (deployment, for you):** nothing in DEPLOY.md or the repo records that `TZ=America/Toronto` was actually verified on Vercel post-deploy; the new [schedule-resolve] log prints the server TZ on every plan — after the next deploy, run one plan and check the function logs (`TZ=(unset)` = the DEPLOY.md step was missed; symptoms would be tomorrow-8am-style shifts, a separate bug from this one). Verified: timeLabels 8/8 under default AND UTC runners, schedule 24/24, all 12 suites green, mock e2e 16/16, tsc clean; live on :3200 "plan a lunch" at 2:39 AM → same-day 12:30 PM itinerary, log line `time_window="unspecified" categories=["lunch"] resolved=…12:00 EDT`.

## Fix: unstarted reroute re-anchored the whole itinerary to the current clock
Goal: cancelling a mid leg on a NOT-yet-started itinerary (planned "dinner and drinks after" → 7:33 PM) re-anchored every stop to the wall clock (~1 PM) — diagnose from logs, then make committed start times the source of truth: a reroute reflows only what's genuinely downstream of the break, never re-deriving the schedule from `now`.
What should be done: log at reroute time the committed starts, floor, `now`, affected set, and post-reroute starts; show where the original start gets lost; fix so stops at/before the disruption point keep their committed times whether or not the itinerary was Started; test the unstarted case and confirm the Started (floor-from-active-stop) case still works; verify live.
What was done (technical): permanent `[reroute-apply]` log (leg, now, floor, committed starts, affected set; post-commit starts). The log pinned a two-part cause: (1) the affected-set computation NEVER consulted `disruption.legIndex` — it swept in every unlocked stop after the floor, and with no active/locked stop `floorTime` collapses to `now`, so ALL stops (including ones upstream of the break) were "affected" (`floor=now, affected=[0,1,2]`); (2) with stop 0 affected there is no previous kept stop, so `chainStart = floor + 0` = the wall clock — buildSchedule rebuilt the whole evening from 13:03. The Started path masked both (floor = active stop's end = the natural anchor). Fix: affected = timed positions ≥ legIndex+1 (strictly downstream of the broken leg) ∩ after-floor ∩ unlocked; the replanned chain departs at max(floor, previous kept stop's COMMITTED end) — the clock only wins once it has actually overtaken the plan — and `anchor_time` (that departure instant) is returned alongside `floor_time`, with the banner now showing the anchor ("Replanned from 9:18 PM", not "from 5:58 PM" = now). Blast radius deliberately narrowed as part of this: cancelling bar→dessert no longer replaces the BAR (it's upstream and reachable exactly as planned) — the old "deeper chain" test pinned the over-replanning and was updated to the corrected contract (upstream kept, one leg fetched, transit excluded on the dead pair, dessert reflows from the bar's committed end). New test: UNSTARTED itinerary, cancel leg 0 at 13:03 → dinner keeps its committed 19:00–20:45 byte-for-byte, bar+dessert replan anchored at 20:45, `anchor_time` = 20:45; all pre-existing floor/locked/home-leg guarantees green (8/8 reroute, 130 across 12 suites, tsc clean, 16/16 mock e2e). Live on :3200: unstarted "dinner and drinks after" → cancel Union→drinks leg → Union holds 7:33–9:18 PM, only drinks replans (9:25 struck → 9:23 settled), banner "Replanned from 9:18 PM"; then dev-time to mid-dinner (Union active/"now") → cancel again → floor = active end, dinner untouched, "your dinner's unchanged". The [reroute-apply] trail shows `affected=[1]` and dinner's committed start identical before/after in both modes.

## Fix: time-swap regressions — compound requests, explicit meridiem, model-drift guard
Goal: three reported regressions from the am/pm fix — "make it start at 6pm" allegedly routed to duration, explicit "6am" allegedly rejected, and repeated swaps drifting a stop to ~10am — diagnosed from the [swap-apply] log before fixing, since the symptoms had different causes.
What should be done: log classified intent + before/after start/duration for each repro; show the divergence; then fix so "start at 6pm" phrasings classify as time (start 18:00, duration unchanged), "6am" is honored as 06:00 (assume-PM only for bare times), and multi-swap sequences stay coherent; regression tests for the exact phrasings.
What was done (technical): a temporary diagnostic harness ran every phrase through every layer (deterministic parsers, interpretRefinement with a deliberately-lying Groq stub, full swapStop sequences). Findings: the exact phrase "make it start at 6pm" was CLEAN at every layer, and "6am" was parsed/honored correctly everywhere (a 6 AM breakfast swap landed; the dinner refusal is the plausibility band working as previously specified, message intact) — but two adjacent real holes existed. (1) COMPOUND requests: "start at 6pm for 2 hours" parsed BOTH duration and time, and duration-wins silently dropped the time half — the reported "became 2 hours instead of starting at 6pm" symptom. (2) MODEL DRIFT: for relative times the model's delta beat the local parse, accepting garbage (-540 for "an hour earlier" → 19:00 − 9h = 10:00 AM, the reported drift) and sign flips (+30 for "a bit earlier"). Also found while tracing: parseTimeExpr("make it 2 hours") matched "make it 2" as 14:00 (masked by duration-wins — a landmine for the compound fix). Fixes: parseTimeExpr gained a duration-unit negative lookahead (a number owned by "hours/minutes" is never a clock time), a lone-number rule ("6" → 18:00), and `vague: true` markers on non-exact relatives; interpretRefinement routes BOTH-parse compounds to time carrying the duration half, keeps local-absolute-wins, and now makes exact local relatives win outright while vague ones accept model refinement only same-sign and ≤180 min; timeChange applies a compound's duration half via the extracted `resolveNewTotal` (shared with durationChange — 6h cap and category minimum enforced on compounds too), reason reads "Moved dinner to 6:00 PM for 2 hours"; mockSwapDeps and the test harness mirror the routing. Tests: 5 new suites-worth in swap.test.ts — REGRESSION A (exact phrasings are time, "make it 2 hours" is NOT a clock time), A2 (compound applies both halves), B ("6am"/"start at 6am"/"move it to 6am" → 06:00; bare "6"/"at 6" → 18:00; a 6 AM breakfast swap lands), C (4-step composed sequence pinned step-by-step, no drift), and an interpret-floor suite where a lying model (misclassification, meridiem flip, -540 garbage, sign flip) loses every time while a sane refinement (-45 for "a bit earlier") is still accepted — 32/32 swap, 129 across 12 suites, tsc clean, 16/16 mock e2e. Live on :3200 through real Groq: 2 hours → start at 6pm (6:00–8:00 PM, duration kept) → an hour earlier (5:00–7:00) → a bit later (5:30–7:30), and "move it to 6am" on a breakfast plan landed 6:00–7:45 AM; the [swap-apply] trail shows every transition with total=120 stable.

## Fix: venue swaps preserve a customized duration
Goal: close the flagged gap — a venue swap ("cheaper") reset a customized duration ("make it 2 hours") back to the category default, inconsistent with time-swaps after the previous fix.
What should be done: in the venue-swap path, build the replacement stop with the existing stop's customized duration instead of recomputing from the category default; rule for the judgment call: preserve when the category is unchanged, fall back to the new category's default when the swap changes the kind of place; tests for both branches; live-verify 2 hours → cheaper → still 2 hours.
What was done (technical): `finalize` (the shared write-back for refilter/research venue swaps) now computes `total = category === target.category ? target.durationMinutes?.total ?? defaultTotal : defaultTotal`, with the base/buffer split mirroring `buildStop`'s override semantics (buffer preserved via `min(defaultBuffer, total)`, base absorbs the customization) so `base + buffer === total` stays consistent; the downstream-overflow shift keys off the preserved end automatically. Existing tests were unaffected by construction (their fixture durations equal the defaults, so preserve-vs-recompute was previously indistinguishable). Two new swap tests: duration-swap dinner to 120 then "somewhere cheaper" → new venue, held 19:00 start, end 21:00, total 120 with buffer 15/base 105, downstream already settled (no double shift); duration-swap the bar to 120 then a research swap to category "restaurant" → total 105 (the NEW category's default) and the shorter stop doesn't yank the downstream earlier. 27/27 swap, tsc clean, 16/16 mock e2e. Live-verified on :3200 through real APIs: Union extended 7:33–9:33 PM, then "somewhere cheaper" swapped to Bar Libretto still 7:33–9:33 PM; the `[swap-apply]` trail shows `total=120` through both swaps. CLAUDE.md's "known gap" flag replaced with the resolved rule: a swap keeps the customized duration wherever the category is unchanged; only category changes (user research swaps or engine adaptations) reset to the new default.

## Fix: duration survives time-swaps + meridiem parsing, plus landing copy
Goal: two locally-found time-swap bugs — a customized duration ("make it 2 hours") was lost by a later time-swap ("an hour earlier" reverted the stop to the category default), and "move it to 6pm" scheduled 6:00 AM — plus landing-page copy changes.
What should be done: add a swap-apply log (stop's start/duration before vs after + parsed intent), diagnose from the logs — no guess-fixes; then make the stop's customized duration the source of truth through subsequent time-swaps (resettleTail already preserves downstream durations); fix parseTimeExpr so meridiems apply ("6pm"→18:00, "9am"→09:00, 12pm→noon, 12am→midnight, bare "3:30"→15:30 on an outing planner) with unit tests for each exact case; verify the compose case (2 hours → earlier keeps 2 hours) and the absolute case (6pm lands 6 PM); remove the tagline + "Ossington, Toronto · tonight", retitle to "Plan your day".
What was done (technical): permanent `[swap-apply]` log in `swapStop` (refinement, intent, parsed time/duration, before/after start+total — or the refusal). The logs confirmed both causes before fixing. BUG 1: `timeChange` computed `anchorEnd` from `getDuration(category)` and called `buildStop` with no `durationOverride`, so the anchor was rebuilt at the category default; fixed with `anchorTotal` = the stop's own `durationMinutes.total` when the venue is kept (adapted replacements still reset to default — same convention as resettleTail), used for both `anchorEnd` and the `buildStop` override. Log now shows `before total=120 | after total=120` across the compose. BUG 2 had two layers: (a) `parseTimeExpr` required a preposition from (after|at|by|around|make it) — "move it to 6pm" matched nothing, so the deterministic floor returned null and the model's misparse ("06:00") sailed through; (b) even when local parsed, the merge preferred the model's syntactically-valid time. Rewrote the absolute section as three ordered matchers — explicit meridiem ANYWHERE ("6pm"/"6 pm"/"12:30am"; pm<12→+12, 12am→0), preposition-anchored bare time with "to" added ("move it to 6", assume-PM 1–11), bare colon time ("3:30"→15:30) — each falling through on out-of-range values so "by 30 minutes" still reads as relative; and flipped the merge so a locally-parsed ABSOLUTE time always beats the model (vague relatives still let the model refine, e.g. word-number amounts). Landing copy: tagline + kicker removed, title → "Plan your day" (no e2e referenced the old copy). Tests: 3 new suites-worth of cases in swap.test.ts — 9 meridiem/bare-time parses incl. both 12 o'clock edges and the "to" phrasing, the COMPOSE case (120 then −60 → start 18:00, total stays 120, downstream chains off the preserved end), the ABSOLUTE case through swapStop — 25/25 swap, 122 across 12 suites, tsc clean, 16/16 mock e2e. Live-verified on :3200 through the REAL Groq interpret: "make it 2 hours" → 7:33–9:33 PM; "an hour earlier" → 6:33–8:33 PM (2h kept); "move it to 6pm" → 6:00–8:00 PM (PM applied AND the custom duration survived a third swap); server log trail shows all three transitions. Known deliberate behavior spotted while fixing (not changed): a VENUE swap ("cheaper") still resets a customized duration to the category default — flagged for a product decision.

## Infra: Vercel deployment prep (persistent store seam)
Goal: deployable to Vercel so a mentor can use it via URL without sharing API keys — with an honest answer to whether the in-memory itinerary store survives serverless.
What should be done: confirm `npm run build` passes and no `.env` is committed; assess the globalThis store under serverless and, if broken, fix with the minimum change behind the same store.ts interface (a seam, like the mock layer); document every env var incl. the Maps key referrer restriction; write deploy + live-verification steps.
What was done (technical): the store DOES break on Vercel — the build output shows every API route as its own dynamic serverless function, so the POST that stores an itinerary and the GET/swap/reroute that read it never share `globalThis`; the Start → swap → reroute flow would 404 immediately. Fix: `loadItinerary`/`saveItinerary` in `store.ts` — with `KV_REST_API_URL`/`KV_REST_API_TOKEN` (or the `UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_*` names) set they speak the Upstash Redis REST API via plain fetch (no new dependency; `["SET", key, json, "EX", 7d]` / `["GET", key]`; Redis is ALWAYS read in KV mode — a per-instance memory copy could be stale the moment another instance writes); without them they collapse to the old Map, so dev and mock e2e are unchanged. On Vercel without KV they throw a loud "no persistent store configured" instead of silent 404s. The four itinerary routes now load → mutate → `saveItinerary` (write-back also on GET and on swap/reroute refusals, since `withStatuses`' lock ratchet mutates); engines untouched. Also: `/api/weather` gained `export const dynamic = "force-dynamic"` (a parameterless GET would otherwise be executed and cached at BUILD time; its inner fetch keeps the 10-min data cache), and the UTC-serverless scheduling gotcha (schedule.ts date math is server-local Toronto by documented assumption) is handled by requiring `TZ=America/Toronto` in the Vercel env — zero code change. New `store.kv.test.ts` (5 cases, Redis stubbed via fetch like the Groq stubs): memory mode has zero Redis traffic and returns the same object; KV save issues SET with `itin:` key + TTL; KV load always hits Redis and round-trips priceLevel/description; unknown id → undefined; serverless-without-KV rejects loudly. Verified: tsc clean, 112 unit tests across 11 suites, `npm run build` clean (all API routes ƒ dynamic), full 16-test mock e2e green through the new load/save path. `DEPLOY.md` documents the env table (5 keys + TZ + KV pair), the Maps-key referrer restriction (`https://<project>.vercel.app/*`), repo steps (Root Directory = itinerary; the folder was uncommitted), and a 6-step live verification checklist that deliberately crosses serverless functions (incl. a cold-start recheck after ~10 min).

## Infra: e2e scenario tests for the bug fixes (mock fixtures)
Goal: lock in the fail-loud/constraint/price/UI fixes — and the interacting-state behaviors that passed manual testing — as fast, deterministic Playwright specs so they're guarded going forward.
What should be done: @mock-tagged specs reusing planEvening and expectStripMatchesPin: every fail-loud message pinned exact-text ("brunch at 3am", "dinner at 4am", "cheap fancy dinner", "asdfghjkl", "."); constraint enforcement both ways ("vegan steakhouse" honest failure, "vegan dinner" evidenced pick); the price indicator changing $$$ → $$ on a cheaper swap with the desync check holding; description present vs absent; swap input accepting spaces via real keystrokes; the empty-pool net via a weather-precip fixture trigger; plus repeated swaps on one stop, swap-then-reroute consistency, and active-stop swap rejection.
What was done (technical): three fixture nudges — `venue()` desc is now optional (omitted `editorialSummary`, keep-on-missing) and Sundown Scoops is the deliberate absent-description case; `mockWeather` grew to 48 hours with a built-in daily rain window at 3 PM local (`MOCK_RAIN_HOUR`, precip 80) so "a walk in the park at 3pm" deterministically weather-blocks whether it resolves today or rolls to tomorrow; `mockParse` learned walk/park → "park walk". Two new helpers in `e2e/helpers.ts`: `planExpectingProblem` (the planEvening inverse — waits for the fail-loud surface, asserts NO `.lstrip` renders behind it, returns the message for exact-text pinning) and `swapOn` (select card → type refinement → submit; callers assert the outcome). `e2e/failloud.spec.ts`: the five bad-input messages exact-text (parametrized), the rained-out empty-pool net, "vegan steakhouse" → the exact unmet-constraint message, "vegan dinner" → Noodle Letterpress + desync check. `e2e/scenarios.spec.ts`: price refresh (Velvet Fig $$$ → "cheaper" → The Corner Table $$, `.lstrip__price` exact + desync on both stops); description renders for Velvet Fig/Curfew and is absent for Sundown Scoops; `pressSequentially` proves real space keystrokes land ("a bit cheaper", not "abitcheaper"); repeated swaps cycle deterministically (cheaper → Corner $$, fancier → Velvet $$$ back, cheaper → Corner $$ — previous venue excluded, one-before is fair game) with desync after each; swap-then-reroute uses the dev time-sim (`simAt` mirrors resolveStartTime's 19:00 roll-forward) to lock the swapped stop mid-dinner, cancels leg 0, and asserts the banner ("cancelled. Replanned from … your dinner's unchanged"), the locked Corner Table keeps its "now", and both surfaces stay in sync; active-stop rejection asserts the live card offers no swap input while the upcoming one does. Suite: 16 tests (2 existing + 14 new), all @mock except smoke, 16/16 passed twice back-to-back (~30s, workers:1, :3100, no live quota); tsc clean; unit suites still green (fixture changes are additive). `e2e/README.md` updated (new spec files, helpers, Sundown + rain-window fixture notes).

## Fix: fail-loud surface for bad input + constraint enforcement + price refresh + 2 UI items
Goal: four manual-testing bugs shared one root cause — degenerate/impossible/contradictory input fell through to an empty map or a borrowed error instead of one honest "couldn't do that, here's why, try this" surface — plus constraints leaked "may accommodate" hedges, cheaper swaps didn't refresh the dollar signs, the swap input ate spaces, and cards lacked a venue description.
What should be done: ONE fail-loud surface (clear reason + suggested fix) for every bad input: impossible explicit times ("brunch at 3am", "dinner at 4am") get a specific category-window message instead of an empty map; contradictions ("cheap fancy dinner") are detected and named; gibberish ("asdfghjkl", ".") gets "I couldn't understand that", never a time error; hard constraints are enforced — return venues that actually meet them or fail loud, never a pick with a "check with the venue" hedge; the strip's dollar signs must reflect a swapped venue's actual price; the inline swap input must accept spaces; a one-line venue description goes under the stars/price row.
What was done (technical): new pure `app/lib/planGuards.ts` — `degeneratePromptReason` (pre-parse: <3 letters, keyboard-row mash — nonsense never reaches Groq), `emptyParseReason` (all-unspecified parse signature → unparseable, its own branch so time is never blamed), `contradictionReason` (cheap↔fancy signals across prompt+parsed, with "nothing fancy" negation handling), `noVenuesReason`/`weatherBlockedReason` (the all-pools-empty net — no more empty maps), `unmetConstraintReason`. `resolveStartTimeChecked` now validates EVERY resolved start (explicit clock times and day-parts included) against the plausible bands: out-of-band explicit times fail with "Couldn't plan a 3 AM brunch — brunch around here runs about 8 AM to 3 PM. Try a later time?" (direction-aware, wrap-band safe); inferred starts keep the add-a-time message. `runPipeline` wires all guards in order (degenerate → parse → unparseable → contradiction → time band → all-empty-pool net → unmet constraint) through one `fail(reason)` path. Constraint enforcement: `selectVenues`' contract changed — hard constraints need actual evidence (data/desc/name/known character); no candidate meets one → the model returns `id:null + unmet_constraint` (accepted as VALID by `findProblems`, no retry/fallback papering); a code-side `HEDGE_PATTERN` backstop converts any hedged pick ("worth confirming", "may accommodate") into the same unmet failure when constraints are stated; `candidateView` now sends `desc` (editorialSummary) as evidence; the swap engine's refusal names the unmet constraint. Price/description: `places.editorialSummary` added to the field mask; `Selection`/`SelectionLike`/`ItineraryStop` carry `priceLevel` + `description` end-to-end (select assembly, swap `buildStop`/`finalize`, reroute via the buildSchedule spread), and the strip renders `stop.priceLevel` FIRST — the pools lookup goes stale the moment a swap picks a venue that was never in them. UI: the stop card's role="button" keydown now ignores events not targeting the card itself (its preventDefault on bubbled space keystrokes was eating the swap input's spaces); `.lstrip__desc` one-line italic ellipsis under the facts row. Fixtures mirror all of it deterministically: every fixture venue got a description ("vegan" evidence on Noodle Letterpress, "patio" on The Standing Room), `mockSelect` enforces constraints token-wise and attaches priceLevel/description, `mockParse` returns the honest all-unspecified signature for unrecognized prompts (plus brunch/steakhouse/fancy signals) — the guard scenarios run without live APIs. Tests: 107 green across 10 suites (new planGuards suite; schedule's "explicit times always pass" flipped intentionally to the band contract; select gained honest-null/hedge-conversion/guard-off-without-constraints cases), tsc clean, mock e2e (smoke + fixtures) green. Live-verified on :3200 (port 3000 untouched), actual messages: "brunch at 3am" → the 8 AM–3 PM window message; "dinner at 4am" → the 11 AM–11 PM one; "cheap fancy dinner" → "That's a bit contradictory…"; "asdfghjkl" and "." → "I couldn't make sense of that…"; "vegan steakhouse" → picked The Hogtown Vegan (actually vegan, hedge-free reason); stacked impossible constraints → "Couldn't find a … that's really strictly kosher certified, halal, and vegan — want to drop a constraint…"; "a cheaper spot" swap re-rendered price+description from the stop's own carried fields; space keydown on the swap input no longer defaultPrevented.

## Feature: home origin (leg 0 from Chestnut Residence)
Goal: itineraries start from the user's home — hardcoded to UofT Chestnut Residence (89 Chestnut St, 43.6547, -79.3862) — instead of materializing at the first venue.
What should be done: prepend a home → stop 1 travel leg using the existing computeRoutes logic (transit-first, walk fallback, +5 min margin, short-leg relabel all unchanged); the resolved start becomes the leave-home time and stop 1 starts after the leg; home is an origin waypoint, not a stop (no duration, no card, excluded from stop count/statuses/completion); render a distinct house marker and a "from Home · Chestnut Residence — mode · X min" line; reroute must never touch leg 0, without special-casing the engine; venue search stays anchored on Ossington.
What was done (technical): new `app/api/schedule/home.ts` with the HOME constant, HOME_LEG_INDEX sentinel (-1), and pure `splitHomeLeg` that splits `getTravelLegs([HOME, ...venues])` output into the home leg and re-indexed inter-stop legs. `buildSchedule` gained an optional `homeLeg` param: startISO = leave-home time, first timed stop starts `homeLeg.totalMinutes` later (mirrors the reroute engine's floor + inbound anchoring). The itinerary store persists `homeLeg` as a separate field — deliberately outside `stops` and `legs`, so status derivation, completion, the disruption dropdown, and the reroute engine's leg-index math structurally never see it (no special-case needed; a reroute-after-departure test pins it byte-identical). PlacesTest sends home as point 0 of the same travel request (single-stop plans now get a home leg too) and renders the leg-0 line; ItineraryMap gained a `home` prop with a dark house-glyph pin and the same solid-walk/dashed-transit leg styling. Live run confirmed a genuine named transit leg 0: 505 Dundas, 11 stops, boarding at Dundas St W at Chestnut St, ~28 min incl. margin. 75 tests green across 8 suites (4 new home tests, +2 schedule, +1 itinerary, +1 reroute).

## Fix: rolled-forward schedules show the date
Goal: tomorrow's 10:30 must not read identically to today's — rolled-forward (or any non-today) stops need a date on the "be here" line.
What should be done: per-stop, when a resolved start is not today, prefix the time with a date ("tomorrow" for +1 day, else a short "Sat Jul 6"); same-day stays time-only. Decide per resolved instant, not per itinerary, so a schedule crossing midnight labels only the post-midnight stops. Banner floor time and home-leg leave time follow the same rule. Frontend + formatting only — no scheduling logic changes.
What was done (technical): new pure module `app/lib/timeLabels.ts` — `datePrefix` (calendar-day offset vs a reference now → "" / "tomorrow, " / "Sat Jul 6, "), `formatStopTime` (single labeled instant), `formatStopRange` (the "be here" range, prefix keyed off the START only so a stop straddling midnight stays today-labeled). Wired into the "be here" line, the reroute banner's floor time, and a home-leg "leave by" time (= first timed stop's start − home leg travel). Live-verified: "brunch tomorrow" → "be here tomorrow, 10:22 AM – 12:07 PM" / "leave by tomorrow, 10:00 AM"; "dinner tonight" → "be here 8:29 PM – 10:14 PM" / "leave by 8:00 PM" (time only). 82 tests green across 9 suites (7 new timeLabels tests, incl. the crossing-midnight case).

## Feature: real product UI (printed city-guide over a map)
Goal: replace the `PlacesTest` dev harness with the actual product page — one executable evening in Ossington rendered as printed cartography, with the reroute-healing moment as the emotional centre.
What should be done: full-bleed custom-styled map as the canvas; venue chips at pins that expand into editorial cards; ink-navy route lines (dashed transit, solid walk) with transit-line leg labels; a distinct home marker with a "leave by" time; a prominent empty-state prompt that recedes to a slim bar; the reroute reflow as the single orchestrated animation (dim cancelled leg, strike old times, settle new times in acid green, chartreuse "now"/changed markers, redrawn route, plain-voice banner); acid green reserved strictly for live/changed; dev time + disruption controls kept as a discreet strip; pipeline wiring untouched; responsive + reduced-motion + visible focus.
## Infra: pipeline mocking seam (deterministic e2e)
Goal: e2e tests shouldn't burn Groq/Places/Routes quota or vary run to run — a fixture mode that swaps the pipeline's data sources for canned, deterministic responses, keeping every piece of real logic running.
What should be done: an E2E_MOCK=1 env flag; fixtures rich enough for real scenarios (varied prices so "cheaper" swaps land, varied hours so time-swaps hit open/closed, 3+ categories, an early-closing venue for the adapt path); a seam not a rewrite (real routes stay default); Playwright mocks by default with a separate live command; smoke passes in mock mode; never touch the manually-tested server on :3000.
What was done (technical): `app/api/_mock/fixtures.ts` holds the flag (`isMockMode()`), the fixture pools, and mock implementations for every data source. Pools: dinner (Velvet Fig 4.8/$$$ top pick — a "cheaper" swap must beat it; The Corner Table $$; Noodle Letterpress $; Early Bird Diner closes 20:00), drinks (Ten O'Clock Curfew 4.7 closes 22:00 — the adapt trigger; The Standing Room + Paper Lantern open to 2 AM), dessert (Sundown Scoops closes 21:00; Midnight Flour open to 1 AM), plus generated generic pools for unknown categories; all with real Google-shape `currentOpeningHours` so `isOpenAt`/the hours filter run for real. Seven seam points, each a data-source swap only: parse route (keyword-scan `mockParse`, schema-complete), places/search route (fixture pools INTO the real `filterPools`), select route (`mockSelect`: highest-rated, cheap budget prefers non-$$$), travel route (haversine-derived legs — short hops walk, the cross-town home leg comes out "505 Fixture" transit), weather route (24 calm hours), and both engines' `realDeps()` (swap gets `mockSwapDeps` — deterministic interpret built on the exported `parseTimeExpr`/`parseDurationExpr` (injected to avoid an import cycle) + a fixture-registry `isUsableAt` so the adapt path genuinely fires on stored stops; reroute gets `mockRerouteDeps`). Playwright default config now runs MOCK on port 3100 (webServer env injects E2E_MOCK=1; :3000 never referenced); `playwright.live.config.ts` + `npm run test:e2e:live` runs real APIs on :3000 excluding `@mock`-tagged tests. New `e2e/fixtures.spec.ts` (@mock) guards the seam by pinning the deterministic picks (Velvet Fig + Ten O'Clock Curfew), the fixture transit line, and the canned weather. Verified: tsc clean, all 104 unit tests green (E2E_MOCK unset → real paths untouched), mock e2e passed twice with identical results (smoke dropped from ~6–10s live to ~1.5s), port 3000 untouched throughout.

## Infra: Playwright e2e foundation
Goal: scaffold end-to-end testing so specific scenarios can be automated later — no feature suite yet, just prove the harness and the one assertion that matters.
What should be done: install Playwright against the dev server on :3000; one smoke test (plan "dinner and drinks", assert both stop cards render with names and times); one reusable desync helper (a stop's strip-card time must match its map-pin time — the strip/map/store agreement check); confirm headless + headed; document the commands.
What was done (technical): `@playwright/test` + Chromium installed; `playwright.config.ts` reuses an already-running dev server (starts `npm run dev` if none — never `next build`), runs serially (workers: 1 — live pipeline + in-memory store), pipeline-sized timeouts, trace on failure. `e2e/helpers.ts`: `planEvening(page, prompt)` drives the empty state through the live pipeline and fails fast with the app's own error text; `expectStripMatchesPin(page, venueName)` extracts each surface's effective start label (settled `.new-time` when a swap/reroute just changed it, else the "be here" range start / the chip time — both render through formatStopTime, so string equality is the contract) and asserts they match. `e2e/smoke.spec.ts` is the only spec. Verified headless AND headed (both pass, ~6-10s test), plus a one-off negative proof: sabotaging a pin's DOM time made the helper throw "desynced" — it genuinely catches mismatches (temp spec deleted after the run). Commands: `npm run test:e2e` / `npm run test:e2e:headed` (documented in `e2e/README.md`). Artifacts gitignored (`test-results/`, `playwright-report/`).

## Feature: duration swaps (third swap type)
Goal: handle "how long" ("stay 2 hours", "stay longer", "shorter", "just an hour") as a third swap intent, reusing the existing try→adapt→notify ladder.
What should be done: add intent "duration" with a deterministic `parseDurationExpr` floor (absolute "stay 2 hours" = 120, relative "stay longer" = +30, "an extra hour" = +60, "shorter" = -30); change the stop's duration (end = start + new duration, start stays), then reuse `resettleTail` to reflow/adapt downstream; sane bounds (floor at the category's realistic minimum so "10 minute dinner" fails, cap at 6h so "stay 20 hours" fails); product-voice banner; reuse `isUsableAt` (a longer stay just means later downstream arrivals, already covered). Do NOT write new tail-rechaining or availability logic.
What was done (technical): `SwapInterpretation.duration` = `{ mode: "relative"|"absolute", deltaMinutes?, targetMinutes? }`, with `parseDurationExpr` (exported, unit-tested) as the floor under the Groq call — duration wins over time, and a bare time direction with no duration word ("an hour earlier") is explicitly NOT read as a duration. New `durationChange` computes the new total from the stop's current duration, guards it (categoryMin = max(15, 40% of the category default) so "10 minute dinner" fails; cap 360 so "stay 20 hours" fails), then calls the SAME `resettleTail` used by time-swaps. `buildStop` gained a `durationOverride` (custom total, category buffer preserved); `resettleTail`/`commitTail` now carry each stop's `totalMinutes` so a kept stop's duration survives a later reflow (adapted stops take the category default). Availability seam and the ladder are untouched — no duplication. Banner in product voice: "Extended dinner to 2 hours — everything after shifted later" (adds "(moved a later stop to a spot that's still open)" when it adapts). Tests: 8 new cases (parseDurationExpr; "stay 2 hours" extend+reflow; "stay longer" +30; "shorter" -30; extension that closes a later venue adapts; extension nothing absorbs fails loud; "stay 20 hours" + "10 minute dinner" both fail loud; locked downstream never moves) — 22 swap cases, 104 green across 10 suites, tsc clean. Live-verified: "stay 2 hours" extended dinner 8:29→10:29 PM and reflowed the bar (strike→settle); "stay longer" took it to 2h 30m and reflowed again.

## Fix: time-swap intent + inline swap prompt
Goal: "an hour earlier" returned "couldn't tell what time you meant" — relative time expressions didn't parse. Fix the time path (venue swaps already worked), and move the swap prompt off the detached bottom bar into the selected strip card.
What should be done: interpret must handle relative ("an hour earlier" → -60, "a bit later" → +30, vague defaults to a 30-min step) AND absolute ("after 8" → 20:00); apply the shift, then a general try → adapt → notify ladder (shift + reflow via shared chaining, floor_time guard; if a later stop closes at the new timing, re-search an equivalent; only if nothing works, fail with the specific reason). Make the ladder a reusable helper (duration-swaps reuse it next). Move the swap prompt inline under the selected card. Isolate the "is this venue usable at this time" check behind one seam for a future availability API.
What was done (technical): `SwapInterpretation.time` is now `{ mode: "relative"|"absolute", deltaMinutes?, targetTime? }`; a deterministic `parseTimeExpr` (exported, unit-tested) parses relative/absolute expressions and is the floor under the Groq call — a time phrase stays a time request even if the model misclassifies it. The engine's `timeChange` computes the new start, guards (plausible hour → floor → no overlap with the previous stop), resolves the anchor venue (keep if usable at the new time, else adapt, else notify), then calls the new reusable `resettleTail(itinerary, anchorIndex, …, anchorEnd, anchorLoc, …, used)` — the try→adapt→notify ladder that re-chains each downstream stop, keeping its venue when usable and re-searching an equivalent (`findReplacement`) when it closes, failing loud with the real reason if nothing adapts; the whole plan is computed before any mutation, so a failure leaves the itinerary untouched. Availability is isolated behind `SwapDeps.isUsableAt(place, when, category)` (default `usableByHours` via `isOpenAt`, keep-on-missing) — a real availability API replaces that one function body. `bandForCategories` / `isPlausibleAt` were exported from `schedule.ts` for the plausible-hour guard. Frontend: the swap prompt moved into `ItineraryStrip`'s selected card (`SwapInline`), the detached bottom `.swapbar` is gone; the banner no longer double-prefixes time results ("Moved bar to 10:48 PM", not "Swapped bar — Moved bar…"). Also fixed a map crash: the projection probe called `setState` inside `draw()`; it's now deferred to `requestAnimationFrame`. Tests: 14 swap cases (parseTimeExpr relative/absolute/non-time; -60 reflow; +30 default; absolute "after 8"; adapt-on-close; fail-when-nothing-adapts; locked downstream never moves; "dinner at 4am" fails loud) — 96 green across 10 suites, tsc clean. Live-verified end to end: "an hour earlier" moved dinner 8:29 → 7:29 PM and reflowed the bar; "a bit later" moved the bar 10:18 → 10:48 PM. Duration/extend deliberately untouched — the ladder is left generic for it to reuse.

## Feature: per-stop swap
Goal: let the user tap ONE upcoming stop, type a short complaint ("somewhere cheaper", "with a patio"), and replace just that stop with a closer match — surgical, distinct from reroute (which replans the whole downstream tail after an external disruption).
What should be done: an engine that guards to upcoming-only (reuse floor_time), interprets the complaint via a small Groq call into an updated constraint set + a re-filter (narrow the same pool) vs re-search (new query for features/cuisine the pool lacks) classification, excludes the rejected venue, holds the stop's time slot, recomputes its duration + outbound leg, and shifts downstream only when the new pick genuinely can't fit — never touching locked/past. Reuse searchPlaces/selectVenues/filter/schedule cores; return a diff; wire a POST route; add an inline swap prompt that reuses the reroute reflow visuals; product-voice copy; honest refusal when nothing better is found.
What was done (technical): shared floor_time by extracting `floorTime(itinerary, now)` into `store.ts` (reroute now calls it too — behaviour identical, its 7 tests unchanged) and exported `toTorontoISO` from `schedule.ts`. New `app/api/itinerary/swap.ts`: injectable `SwapDeps` (interpret / searchPools / selectVenues / getSingleLeg); a Groq `interpretRefinement` returning `{ path, category, aesthetic, budget, constraints }`; refilter searches the ORIGINAL query then judges with the new constraints, research searches with the new query too; rejected + already-used venue ids excluded before select; the new venue holds the old `start_time`, duration recomputed via `getDuration`, inbound (map geometry) and outbound legs re-fetched; fit check shifts the downstream tail by the delta via `toTorontoISO` only when it overflows (locked/past sit before the swapped stop and are never touched); returns `{ swapped, stopIndex, path, before, after, reason, downstreamShifted }`; empty candidates → honest `{ swapped:false, reason:"Couldn't find a better …" }`. `POST /api/itinerary/[id]/swap` wraps it. Frontend: an ink-navy swap bar revealed only when an upcoming stop is selected ("Not quite right? Change <venue>"), `doSwap` reusing the reroute reflow — "changed" is now keyed by venue id (a swap can change a stop's category), the swapped stop gets a chartreuse changed-ring marker (NOT a "now" pin, since it's upcoming) and settles into place, downstream shifts strike-through their old times; banner "Swapped dinner — <reason>". 6 swap unit tests (held slot, downstream shift + locked untouched, rejected never reappears, refilter vs research classified, active/past rejected, nothing-better refusal). Live-verified: "somewhere cheaper" on the dinner swapped Union → Salt Wine Bar Ltd, held the 8:29–10:14 PM slot, chartreuse changed accent; moving the dev clock into the stop made it the active "now" (full chartreuse marker) and hid the swap bar. 88 tests green across 10 suites, tsc clean. Pipeline cores untouched.

## Feature: real product UI (printed city-guide over a map)
Goal: replace the `PlacesTest` dev harness with the actual product page — one executable evening in Ossington rendered as printed cartography, with the reroute-healing moment as the emotional centre.
What should be done: full-bleed custom-styled map as the canvas; venue chips at pins that expand into editorial cards; ink-navy route lines (dashed transit, solid walk) with transit-line leg labels; a distinct home marker with a "leave by" time; a prominent empty-state prompt that recedes to a slim bar; the reroute reflow as the single orchestrated animation (dim cancelled leg, strike old times, settle new times in acid green, chartreuse "now"/changed markers, redrawn route, plain-voice banner); acid green reserved strictly for live/changed; dev time + disruption controls kept as a discreet strip; pipeline wiring untouched; responsive + reduced-motion + visible focus.
What was done (technical): design plan approved as a first pass (rendered specimen — warm paper #E9E6DF, ink-navy #17212E, reserved acid green #C8F000; Fraunces display + Space Grotesk mechanical). Rebuilt `app/globals.css` as the token system + component styles (fonts via Google Fonts import). Rewrote `app/ItineraryMap.tsx` to a classic `google.maps.Map` with inline warm-paper `styles` JSON (POIs/transit stripped) — dropped `DEMO_MAP_ID` because inline styling needs a non-vector map — and an `OverlayView` "projection probe" whose `draw()` feeds live container-pixel coords to a React overlay layer of chips / expanded cards; route `Polyline`s in ink-navy with the changed stop's inbound leg redrawn in chartreuse; transit leg labels at each leg midpoint. Wrote `app/page.tsx` as the product page: ported the whole parse→places→filter→select→travel→schedule→store→reroute orchestration out of the harness, auto-storing the itinerary so live/reroute controls work immediately; empty-state prompt, slim persistent top bar, reroute banner, weather-blocked notes, and the discreet dev strip. Deleted `app/PlacesTest.tsx`. Fixes during verification: fitBounds now keys off a coordinate signature (`fitKey`) so status ticks don't yank the view; expanded cards clamp horizontally and flip below the pin near the top edge; z-order so the active card sits above neighbouring chips; reroute auto-selects the changed stop so its strike-through/settle is the hero. Copy updated to product voice (empty state, banner, weather-blocked, fail-loud `IMPLAUSIBLE_TIME_MESSAGE`). Live-verified end to end: full pipeline renders on the paper map; home leg fires a named "505 Dundas · 11 stops · 29 min" transit leg; setting the dev clock mid-dinner turns the dinner chartreuse ("now"); cancelling the leg replans the bar (Sweaty Betty's → Civil Liberties), strikes 10:21 PM → settles 10:42 PM in acid green, redraws the route in chartreuse, and banners "That leg cancelled. Replanned from tomorrow, 10:14 PM — your dinner's unchanged." Mobile (375px) and desktop both verified; 82 tests green, tsc clean. Pipeline logic untouched.
